    if (path.endsWith('/index')) path = path.slice(0, -6);
    else if (path === 'index') path = '';

    var candidates = [];
    if (path) candidates.push(path);
    if (path.startsWith('/')) candidates.push(path.slice(1));
    else candidates.push('/' + path);

    var item = null;
    for (var i = 0; i < candidates.length && !item; i++) {
      var matches = document.querySelectorAll('[id="' + candidates[i].replace(/"/g, '\\"') + '"]');
      for (var j = 0; j < matches.length; j++) {
        if (matches[j].closest('#sidebar, #sidebar-content')) {
          item = matches[j];
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    if (!item) return;

    var parent = item.closest('[data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport"]');
    if (!parent) {
      parent = item.parentElement;
      while (parent) {
        var style = getComputedStyle(parent);
        if (style.overflowY === 'auto' || style.overflowY === 'scroll') break;
        parent = parent.parentElement;
      }
    }
    if (!parent) return;

    var parentRect = parent.getBoundingClientRect();
    var itemRect = item.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (itemRect.top >= parentRect.top && itemRect.bottom <= parentRect.bottom) return;

    var itemTopRelative = itemRect.top - parentRect.top + parent.scrollTop;
    parent.scrollTop = itemTopRelative - parentRect.height / 2 + itemRect.height / 2;

/* 顶部 banner：使用新版 StepFun 蓝色品牌渐变。 */
#banner {

}

/* ============================================================
 * API 参数列表：字段名高亮（A 方案：蓝色加粗 + 浅蓝底）
 * 现状：参数行 markdown 写法为 `- \`field\` \`type\` **_required_**<br />描述`，
 *       字段名和类型都渲染为同样的灰色 <code>，视觉无法区分。
 * 策略：识别两类「参数行」DOM 模式，给首个 <code>（=字段名）加蓝底加粗：
 *   1. 带描述：li/span 直接子节点含 <br>
 *   2. 容器型：li 直接子节点含 <details>（Mintlify <Expandable> 渲染产物）
 *      —— 用于 image_url / video_url / input_audio 等"无 inline 描述、
 *      只展开子参数"的字段
 * 防误伤：每个 selector 都要求容器内含 `code:first-child + code`（两个相邻 code），
 *   以排除「裸文字 + 单个 code」的标签变体（如「用户消息 `object`」），
 *   这种 li 的 `<code>` 是「type」不是「name」，不应染蓝。
 * 每种模式各有 tight list / loose list（多一层 <span data-as="p">）两个变体。
 * ============================================================ */
li:has(> br):has(> code:first-child + code) > code:first-child,
li > span[data-as="p"]:has(> br):has(> code:first-child + code) > code:first-child,
li:has(> details):has(> code:first-child + code) > code:first-child,
li:has(> details) > span[data-as="p"]:has(> code:first-child + code) > code:first-child {
  color: rgb(37 99 235);
  font-weight: 600;
  background-color: rgb(37 99 235 / 0.08);
}
.dark li:has(> br):has(> code:first-child + code) > code:first-child,
.dark li > span[data-as="p"]:has(> br):has(> code:first-child + code) > code:first-child,
.dark li:has(> details):has(> code:first-child + code) > code:first-child,
.dark li:has(> details) > span[data-as="p"]:has(> code:first-child + code) > code:first-child {
  color: rgb(96 165 250);
  background-color: rgb(96 165 250 / 0.15);
}

href="/docs/zh/step-code/configuration/models">Step Code 登录与认证</a> · <a class="link" href="/docs/zh/guides/pricing/details">按量付费定价与限速</a>。</span>
<span data-as="p">Step Plan 是阶跃星辰开放平台推出的订阅制服务，让您在主流编码工具与智能体平台（如 OpenClaw、Claude Code、Trae、Cursor 等）中，以订阅价格调用阶跃星辰的旗舰模型。订阅后通过有效的 Step API Key 接入，按月获得统一的模型调用额度。</span>
<div role="note" aria-label="注意" class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 dark:border-blue-900 dark:bg-blue-600/20" data-callout-type="note"><div class="mt-0.5 w-4" data-component-part="callout-icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-4 text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300"><path d="M 9 1.6714 C 13.0371 1.6714 16.3286 4.9629 16.3286 9 C 16.3286 13.0371 13.0371 16.3286 9 16.3286 C 7.0574 16.3252 5.1953 15.552 3.8217 14.1783 C 2.448 12.8047 1.6748 10.9426 1.6714 9 C 1.6714 4.9629 4.9629 1.6714 9 1.6714 Z M 9 0 C 4.0371 0 0 4.0371 0 9 C 0 13.9629 4.0371 18 9 18 C 13.9629 18 18 13.9629 18 9 C 18 4.0371 13.9629 0 9 0 Z M 10.2857 3.8571 H 7.7143 V 10.2857 H 10.2857 V 3.8571 Z M 10.2857 11.5714 H 7.7143 V 14.1429 H 10.2857 V 11.5714 Z" fill="currentColor" stroke="none" fill-rule="evenodd" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg></div><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_kbd]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p">Step Plan 已升级为 <strong>Credit 月池</strong> 计费：额度以 Credit 为单位按月发放、月内灵活消耗。若您此前订阅的是旧版按请求次数限额的套餐，请参阅 <a class="link" href="/docs/zh/step-plan/upgrade-notice">Step Plan 升级公告</a> 了解差异与升级方式。</span></div></div>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="主要特性"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#主要特性" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="导航到标题">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">主要特性</span></h2>
<ul>
<li><strong>统一计费单位</strong>：以 Credit 衡量所有模型的用量，无需分别记忆每个模型的价格。</li>
<li><strong>默认高速模型体验</strong>：所有档位均提供统一的高速模型性能，不对速度额外收费。</li>
<li><strong>月池额度，灵活消耗</strong>：Credit 按月一次性发放，月内任意时段消耗，不受时段或请求频次限制。</li>
<li><strong>超额可加购</strong>：当月额度用尽后，可加购加油包补充额度，无需等待次月。</li>
<li><strong>全平台可用</strong>：不限制使用平台，一个订阅覆盖主流编码与智能体工具链。</li>
<li><strong>覆盖多类模型</strong>：涵盖文本、推理、语音与智能路由等模型，并将持续扩展。</li>
</ul>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="计费方式"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#计费方式" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="导航到标题">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">计费方式</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Step Plan 以 <strong>Credit</strong> 作为统一计费单位。调用任意模型时，用量都会换算为 Credit，从当月额度中扣减。</span>
<span data-as="p">Credit 与支付金额按 <strong>1M Credit = ¥1</strong> 换算（即 100 万 Credit 对应 ¥1 的模型用量）。各档位提供的用量高低，取决于其每月发放的 Credit 数量。</span>
<span data-as="p">订阅后，Credit 一次性发放至当月「月池」，<strong>月内任意时段消耗，月末清零、不结转至下一周期</strong>。</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="扣费顺序"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#扣费顺序" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="导航到标题">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">扣费顺序</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">当账户中同时存在月池 Credit 与加油包 Credit 时，按到期时间先后消耗，<strong>优先扣减先到期的额度</strong>。其中月池 Credit 以当月清零日为到期时间，加油包 Credit 以其各自的 30 天到期日为准。</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="套餐档位"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#套餐档位" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="导航到标题">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">套餐档位</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Step Plan 提供四个档位，区别在于每月发放的 Credit 额度与价格；各档位均提供统一的高速模型性能。</span>

<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="加油包"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#加油包" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="导航到标题">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">加油包</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">当月月池 Credit 用尽后，可加购加油包补充额度，无需等待次月发放。加油包仅向已订阅 Step Plan 的用户开放，周期单独 30 天，与套餐到期时间相互独立。</span>

<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="支持的模型"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#支持的模型" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="导航到标题">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">支持的模型</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Step Plan 当前支持以下模型，覆盖文本、推理、语音与智能路由等场景。各模型的能力说明与参数详见<a class="link" href="/docs/zh/guides/models/overview">模型能力总览</a>。</span>
<ul>
<li><a class="link" href="/docs/zh/guides/models/step-5-preview"><code>step-5-preview</code></a>：面向真实任务的新一代旗舰基模</li>
<li><a class="link" href="/docs/zh/guides/models/step-3.7-flash"><code>step-3.7-flash</code></a>：高速多模态推理模型</li>
<li><a class="link" href="/docs/zh/guides/models/step-3.5-flash"><code>step-3.5-flash</code></a>：面向智能体与代码任务的高速模型</li>
<li><code>step-3.5-flash-2603</code>：针对高频 Agent 场景优化的版本</li>
<li><a class="link" href="/docs/zh/guides/models/stepaudio-2.5-realtime"><code>stepaudio-2.5-realtime</code></a>：端到端实时语音对话模型</li>
<li><a class="link" href="/docs/zh/guides/models/stepaudio-2.5-chat"><code>stepaudio-2.5-chat</code></a>：端到端语音对话大模型</li>
<li><a class="link" href="/docs/zh/guides/models/stepaudio-2.5-tts"><code>stepaudio-2.5-tts</code></a>：新一代 Contextual TTS 模型</li>
<li><a class="link" href="/docs/zh/guides/models/stepaudio-2.5-asr"><code>stepaudio-2.5-asr</code></a>：新一代自动语音识别模型</li>
<li><a class="link" href="/docs/zh/guides/models/step-router"><code>step-router-v1</code></a>：智能路由模型，按任务复杂度自动调度</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">后续将逐步引入更多阶跃旗舰模型。</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="接入与使用"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#接入与使用" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="导航到标题">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">接入与使用</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">获取有效的 Step API Key 后，请根据所用工具选择 Step Plan 的配置地址：</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>Claude Code / Anthropic SDK：</strong> <code>https://api.stepfun.com/step_plan</code></li>
<li><strong>OpenAI SDK 的 Chat Completions 调用：</strong> <code>https://api.stepfun.com/step_plan/v1</code></li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">以上为客户端配置地址（Base URL），不是完整 HTTP 请求地址。完整请求地址请查阅 <a class="link" href="/docs/zh/api-reference/chat/messages-create">Messages API</a> 与对应 API 文档。Claude Code 的配置步骤见 <a class="link" href="/docs/zh/step-plan/integrations/claude-code">Claude Code 接入指南</a>；其他工具请以专属<a class="link" href="/docs/zh/step-plan/quick-start">接入指南</a>为准。</span>
<span data-as="p">使用 Step Plan 通道消耗套餐 Credit，普通 API 通道的额度与其独立。访问普通 API 地址会进入普通 API 通道，是否可用取决于该通道的权限和账户状态；调用成功并不表示消耗了 Step Plan Credit。</span>

0%, #0077a8 52%, #00a9ff 100%) !important;\n}\n\n/* ============================================================\n * API 参数列表：字段名高亮（A 方案：蓝色加粗 + 浅蓝底）\n * 现状：参数行 markdown 写法为 `- \\`field\\` \\`type\\` **_required_**\u003cbr /\u003e描述`，\n *       字段名和类型都渲染为同样的灰色 \u003ccode\u003e，视觉无法区分。\n * 策略：识别两类「参数行」DOM 模式，给首个 \u003ccode\u003e（=字段名）加蓝底加粗：\n *   1. 带描述：li/span 直接子节点含 \u003cbr\u003e\n *   2. 容器型：li 直接子节点含 \u003cdetails\u003e（Mintlify \u003cExpandable\u003e 渲染产物）\n *      —— 用于 image_url / video_url / input_audio 等\"无 inline 描述、\n *      只展开子参数\"的字段\n * 防误伤：每个 selector 都要求容器内含 `code:first-child + code`（两个相邻 code），\n *   以排除「裸文字 + 单个 code」的标签变体（如「用户消息 `object`」），\n *   这种 li 的 `\u003ccode\u003e` 是「type」不是「name」，不应染蓝。\n * 每种模式各有 tight list / loose list（多一层 \u003cspan data-as=\"p\"\u003e）两个变体。\n * ============================================================ */\nli:has(\u003e br):has(\u003e code:first-child + code) \u003e code:first-child,\nli \u003e span[data-as=\"p\"]:has(\u003e br):has(\u003e code:first-child + code) \u003e code:first-child,\nli:has(\u003e details):has(\u003e code:first-child + code) \u003e code:first-child,\nli:has(\u003e details) \u003e span[data-as=\"p\"]:has(\u003e code:first-child + code) \u003e code:first-child {\n  color: rgb(37 99 235);\n  font-weight: 600;\n  background-color: rgb(37 99 235 / 0.08);\n}\n.dark li:has(\u003e br):has(\u003e code:first-child + code) \u003e code:first-child,\n.dark li \u003e span[data-as=\"p\"]:has(\u003e br):has(\u003e code:first-child + code) \u003e code:first-child,\n.dark li:has(\u003e details):has(\u003e code:first-child + code) \u003e code:first-child,\n.dark li:has(\u003e details) \u003e span[data-as=\"p\"]:has(\u003e code:first-child + code) \u003e code:first-child {\n  color:
